For gradient, need for each lag with weight w_d, add to grad. grad of c_d wrt h_i: derivative h_i(1-h_j) where j=i+d:
- h_i appears as first factor: (1 - h_j) if valid.
- h_i also appears as second factor for terms where i = j+d? i-d valid: term h_{i-d}(1-h_i) derivative wrt h_i = -h_{i-d}.
So for each shift d:
 add (1 - h_{i+d}) to grad_{i} for i=0..n-1-d, and add -h_{i-d} to grad_{i} for i=d..n-1 (for d positive). For d negative similar, but formula if valid:
grad_i += w * (1 - h_{i+d}) if i+d in [0,n-1]
grad_i -= w * h_{i-d} if i-d in [0,n-1]
Because h_i as second factor. Multiply by dx? g_d = dx c_d, so grad includes dx*w.
Thus dF/dh_i = Σ_d weight_d * dx * [ (1 - h_{i+d}) 1_{i+d valid} - h_{i-d} 1_{i-d valid} ]. We can compute efficient by loops over d with vector operations:
grad = zeros(n)
for idx,w in enumerate(weights): if w > eps:
 d = idx - (n-1)
 if d >=0:
   # i=0..n-1-d first
   grad[:n-d] += w * (1 - h[d:])
   # second: i=d..n-1
   grad[d:] -= w * h[:n-d]
 else:
   e = -d
   # i=0..n-e first: i+d valid (i-e) -> h[i-e]
   grad[:n-e] += w * (1 - h[:n-e])
   # second: i=e..n-1: i-d = i+e valid -> h[i+e]
   grad[e:] -= w * h[e:]
This is O(N*n) worst 160k per gradient, okay. But for L-BFGS many iterations maybe. For n=400, gradient ~0.1-1 ms? Python loop 399 with vector ops maybe 5-20 ms. ```
def pair_values(h,n):
    vals = np.empty(2*n-1)
    for idx,d in enumerate(range(-(n-1), n)):
       if d>=0:
          m=n-d
          vals[idx] = np.dot(h[:m], 1.0 - h[d:])
       else:
          m=n+d
          vals[idx] = np.dot(h[-m:], 1.0 - h[:m])
    return vals
```

`corr_custom(h,n)`:
```
def corr_custom(h, n):
    out = np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    m = h
    r = 1.0 - m
    # custom d order from -(n-1) to n-1? Need match final correlation? Max same, but active lags can be based on custom. For final, use np.correlate; sets same. Good.
    idx=0
    for d in range(-(n-1), n):
       if d >= 0:
           e = n - d
           out[idx] = np.dot(h[:e], r[d:])
       else:
           e = n + d
           out[idx] = np.dot(h[-e:], r[:e])
       idx += 1
    return out
```
For d negative, `h[-e:]` where e=n+d, d=-e, i=e..n-1: h[i]*(1-h[i-e]) = h[e:]*r[:e]. Yes.

`grad_lag(h, d, n)`:
Use slices based on custom d:
If d >= 0:
  m = n - d
  g = zeros(n)
  g[:m] += (1 - h[d:])      # as first
  g[d:] -= h[:m]           # as second
If d < 0:
  e = -d; m = n - e
  g[:m] += (1 - h[e:])? Wait custom d=-e: c=Σ_{i=e}^{n-1} h_i (1 - h_{i-e}). First: i in e..n-1, derivative + (1 - h_{i-e}). That's for g[e:] += 1 - h[:m]. Second: for i in 0..m-1 (j=i), term h_{i+e}(1-h_i), derivative -h_{i+e}. So g[:m] -= h[e:].
Let's re-evaluate general formula from earlier for correlate mapping? We need gradient for custom c_d = Σ_{i: i,i+d valid} h_i(1-h_{i+d}). Deriv:
- i as first: for i=0..n-1-d if d≥0 / i=-d..n-1 if d<0: derivative (1-h_{i+d}).
- i as second: for i such that i-d valid: if d≥0, i=d..n-1: derivative -h_{i-d}; if d<0 (e), i=0..n-1-e: derivative -h_{i+e}.
So for d>=0:
  m=n-d
  g[:m] += (1-h[d:])
  g[d:] -= h[:m]
For d<0 e=-d,m=n-e:
  g[e:] += (1-h[:m])
  g[:m] -= h[e:]
This is clean. Multiply by dx if gradient of g (integral). `corr_custom` includes dx; gradient should include dx. We'll compute `g` then `g *= dx`.

```

Constraint fun:
```
def act_fun(x, n, lags):
    h=x[:n]
    t=x[n]
    g=corr_values(h,n)
    out=np.empty(1+len(lags))
    out[0]=h.sum()-n/2.0
    out[1:]=t - g[lags]
    return out
```
`lags` is array/list of indices (0..N-1) or shifts d? Use d list to avoid index conversion. Could pass d list; `corr_values` index = d + n-1. For constraints, `g[idx]`. `act_jac(x,n,ds)`:
```
m=len(ds); q=n+1; J=np.zeros((m+1,q))
J[0,:n]=1.0
h=x[:n]
for r,d in enumerate(ds):
   grad = grad_lag_custom(h,d,n)  # includes dx
   J[r+1,:n] = -grad
   J[r+1,n] = 1.0
return J
```
This calls grad_lag for each active d; O(active*n). Good.

Let's design all helper functions top-level:
1. `compute_c5(h, n)`: use np.correlate max, dx.

3. `project_feasible(h,n)`: projection onto [0,1] and sum target. Can do bisection for exact sum? Previous iterative waterfill not exact if hitting bounds. Need robust. Target n/2 (mean 0.5). Projection of arbitrary h onto box+mean can be solved by finding τ: sum clip(y-τ,0,1)=target. Then y=clip(y-τ). This can be exact (within float). Much better. Implement `project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = n*0.5
    y = h.copy()
    # find tau via bisection over [min(y)-1, max(y)+1]? f(tau)=sum(clip(y-tau,0,1)) decreasing.
    lo = y.min() - 1.0; hi = y.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    yp = np.clip(y-tau,0.0,1.0)
    # adjust tiny residual by modifying an interior element
    diff = target - yp.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # find index with room in direction
       if diff >0:
          room = 1-yp
          idx = int(np.argmax(room))
          if room[idx] > 0: yp[idx] += min(diff, room[idx])
       else:
          idx = int(np.argmax(yp))
          yp[idx] += max(diff, -yp[idx])
    return yp
```
F(τ) monotone; bisection 60 exact. This projection is Euclidean; good. Use after each iteration? SLSQP maintains constraints, but for custom use.
4. `slsqp_active(h0,n,ds,t0,deadline,maxiter,ftol)`: calls minimize. But `minimize` top-level with functions. Need catch. Return h,t.
```
def act_obj(x,n,ds): return float(x[n])
def eq_fun(x,n): return x[:n].sum() - 0.5*n
def eq_jac(x,n):
    out = np.zeros(n+1); out[:n]=1.0; return out
def ineq_fun(x,n,ds):
    h=x[:n]; t=x[n]
    idx = np.asarray(ds, dtype=int) + n - 1
    return t - corr_values(h,n)[idx]
def ineq_jac(x,n,ds):
    m=len(ds); h=x[:n]; J=np.zeros((m,n+1))
    for r,d in enumerate(ds):
        J[r,:n] = -grad_lag(h,d,n)
        J[r,n] = 1.0
    return J
```
`ds` list/tuple. But `np.asarray(ds)` each fun okay.
`grad_lag` top-level:
```
def grad_lag(h,d,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if d>=0:
       m=n-d
       g[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
       g[d:] -= h[:m]
    else:
       e=-d; m=n-e
       g[e:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
       g[:m] -= h[e:]
    g *= dx
    return g
```
Check d=0: m=n; g[:n]+=1-h; g[:n]-=h => 1-2h. Derivative of h(1-h) wrt h_i =1-2h_i, times dx, correct.

def active_optimize(init,n,ds,maxiter,ftol):
    x=np.empty(n+1)
    x[:n]=init; x[n]=float(corr_values(init,n).max()+1e-5)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_fun,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)},
          {'type':'ineq','fun':ineq_fun,'jac':ineq_jac,'args':(n,ds)}]
    res=minimize(act_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=act_obj? no objective grad? Objective linear grad? We can provide jac for objective? minimize accepts `jac` as function returning gradient of objective. If we don't, finite diff of objective (just t) cheap? It will call finite diff maybe; provide top-level `act_obj_jac(x,n,ds): out=zeros(n+1); out[n]=1`.
    res=minimize(act_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=act_obj_jac, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
    if success or feasible: x=res.x; h=project_feasible(x[:n],n); return h, corr_values(h,n).max() maybe.
    else return init.
```

Active ds update in loop:
```
def refine_active(h,n,deadline,kmax=16):
   besth=h.copy(); bestc=compute_c5(h,n)
   g=corr_values(h,n)
   while time.time()<deadline:
       C=bestc
       # select ds: top k by g, also include lags near C
       order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
       # include top 8 plus all with g > C - 0.002? Too many maybe.
       sel_idx = list(order[:8])
       for idx in order:
          if len(sel_idx)>=16: break
          if g[idx] >= C - 0.005 and idx not in sel_idx: sel_idx.append(idx)
       ds = [idx - (n-1) for idx in sel_idx]
       # maybe if no improvement break?
       hh, cc = active_optimize(besth,n,ds,500,1e-12)
       hh=project_feasible(hh,n); hh=project_feasible(hh,n)
       cc=compute_c5(hh,n)
       if cc < bestc - 1e-12:
          besth=hh.copy(); bestc=cc; g=corr_values(hh,n)
       else:
          # try with current g? If no improvement, maybe smaller maxiter/larger K? break or adjust
          # If active constraints not binding? try top 4? Could break.
          if len(sel_idx)>=16: break
          # no improvement, break? To avoid cycles.
          break
   return besth,bestc
```

Pseudo:
```
def run_active(h,n,deadline):
    best=h.copy(); c=compute_c5(best,n); g=corr_values(best,n)
    ks = [8, 12, 18, 24]
    for rep in range(4):
       if time.time()>deadline-30: break
       k = min(len(g), ks[rep % len(ks)])
       order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
       sel = order[:k].copy()
       # include lags within 0.005 of max up to 40? If too many, skip.
       near = np.where(g >= g[0] - 0.004)[0]
       if len(near)<k: sel = np.concatenate([sel, near]) unique
       ds = [(int(i)- (n-1)) for i in sel if ... top limit 32]
       # maybe shuffle near to avoid same? no
       hh,cc=active_optimize(best,n,ds, maxiter=300, ftol=1e-10)
       if cc < bestc - 1e-13: ...
       else:
          # try with fewer (top 5) maybe
```

Let's design binary stochastic search:
- Start from random or parent binary.
- Simulated annealing with target: accept if new max < current; occasional worse. Update counts and h/oms/hrev.

Could use best-improvement on top high lags: Select zero i and one j randomly from contributing terms of current top lag to reduce it. For a top lag d, reductions come from pairs: if d>=0, c_d counts i with h_i=1,h_{i+d}=0. We can reduce by moving a zero j to i? Wait c_d decreases if we set a contributing one j to zero and the zero at j+d to one? For lag d>=0, each term is 1 at i where h_i=1,h_{i+d}=0. To remove it, either make h_i=0 (swap one i with some zero) or make h_{i+d}=1 (swap zero i+d with some one). A single swap i_zero -> one, j_one -> zero: It removes contributions where j is one in c_d (j+d zero) and adds where i zero becomes one (i+d zero). To reduce top lag d, choose j among contributing ones (for positive d, j one with j+d zero) and i among zeros that are at positions where i+d is one (so adds 0? If i+d one, no new contribution; specifically for lag d, adding i contributes if i+d zero). So choose i zero with i+d one. Good heuristic. We can sample such pairs. This targeted search may reach binary target faster.

Implement `binary_target_search(h,n,deadline,target_count)`:
- counts custom, curmax.
- While time:
  - If curmax <= target: return success.
  - Get list of max lags `max_idx = np.where(counts > curmax - 1?)` (counts integer, use ==curmax). Pick random d.
  - Determine candidate j (one to remove) and i (zero to add) as above for that d:
      if d>=0: j in ones with j+d<n and h[j+d]==0; i in zeros with i+d<n and h[i+d]==1. If no, random.
      if d<0: e=-d; c_d counts i=e..n-1 with h_i=1,h_{i-e}=0. To remove, j one with j-e>=0 and h[j-e]==0; add i zero with i-e>=0 and h[i-e]==1.
    More generally for custom d:
      removing j contributes to c_d for terms where j is first (j+d valid and zero) plus? Also j can contribute as second? c_d includes h_i(1-h_i+d). The variable j one can appear as first if j+d valid; if j+d zero, contributes. as second if j-d valid: term h_{j-d}(1-h_j)=0 always because j one, so no. Thus only first role. To remove contribution, choose j with j+d valid and h[j+d]=0 (if d>=0, pos j+d; if d<0, pos j+d exists? d negative => j+d>=0? j+d valid). To add i zero not contributing: need i+d valid and h[i+d]=1 (so new term 1*(1-1)=0). Also if i = j+d etc interactions handled.
  - After moving, recompute delta and new max. Accept if < cur or SIMMETRY. Track best.
This can reduce max specifically.

Let's implement `active_many`:
```
def active_many(h,n,deadline, iters_per=200, alphas=...):
    best=h.copy(); c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # logsum SLSQP warm-up maybe alpha=50 to smooth
    while time.time()<deadline-20:
        g=corr_values(best,n)
        C=g.max()
        # choose active ds top K based on C
        order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        # include top 10
        idxs=list(order[:10].tolist())
        # add near max if count small
        for idx in order[10:]:
            if len(idxs) >= 24: break
            if g[idx] >= C - 0.006:
                idxs.append(int(idx))
        ds=[i-(n-1) for i in idxs]
        hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,300,1e-12)
        hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c=ch; best=hh.copy(); continue
        # If active no improvement, try logsum with moderate alpha
        hh=slsqp_logsum(best, 200, 200, 1e-12)
        hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c=ch; best=hh.copy(); # maybe reset active kits
        else:
            break
    return best,c
```

def active_phase(h,n,deadline):
    best=h.copy(); c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # warm logsum
    if time permitting and c>0.375:
       for alpha in [50,200,1000]:
          if time>deadline-30: break
          hh=slsqp_logsum(best,n,alpha,200,1e-12)
          hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
          if ch < c-1e-11: c,best=ch,hh.copy()
    # active loops
    for k in [8,12,18,24]:
       rounds=2
       for r in range(rounds):
          if time>deadline-20: break
          g=corr_values(best,n); C=g.max()
          order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
          idxs=np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:k], np.where(g>=C-0.005)[0][: k*2]))).tolist()[:min(30, 2*k+6)]
          ds=tuple(i-(n-1) for i in idxs)
          hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,250,1e-12)
          hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
          if ch < c - 1e-12:
             c,best=ch,hh.copy()
          else:
             # try small logsum? maybe break
             break
    # high logsum polish
    for alpha in [3000, 10000, 50000]:
       if time>deadline-10: break
       hh=slsqp_logsum(best,n,alpha,150,1e-13)
       ...
    return best,c
```

def binary_round(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=500):
    h=(h>0.5).astype(float) ??? to_binary to ensure sum
    counts = correlate reverse
    dx=2/n; cur=counts.max(); bestc=cur*dx; besth=h.copy()
    zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0].tolist(); ones=...; oms=1-h; hrev=h[::-1]; delta=np.empty(N); 
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time>deadline: break
       improved=False; accepts=0
       # maybe shuffle candidate order to avoid path?
       for i in zeros:
          if time>deadline: break
          for j in ones:
              delta.fill(0.0)
              # update
              ...
              tmp=counts + delta ? We can avoid tmp allocation by `new = counts + delta`; but this allocates. Use `np.maximum(counts, counts+delta)`? still allocation.
              # Use `tmp = counts.copy()?` too. Could use `np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)` with preallocated tmp.

              nc=tmp.max()

                  counts += delta; cur=nc; update h,oms,hrev, lists; improved=True; accepts+=1; break
          if improved: break
       if not improved: break

    # final recompute to avoid drift
    counts = correlate(h,1-h,'full')[::-1]; bmax=counts.max()
    return h, bmax*dx, bmax
```

Helper details:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```
`h` maybe 1D. Good.

`corr_values`:
```
def corr_values(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float) * (2.0/n)
```
Could `.astype(float)` each call copies. We can avoid if float; but fine. Use `np.asarray`. For performance, no astype? correlate of float returns float. `[::-1]` view, multiply creates array. Good.

`grad_lag`:
```
def grad_lag(h,d,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    if d>=0:
        m=n-d
        g[:m] += 1.0-h[d:]
        g[d:] -= h[:m]
    else:
        e=-d; m=n-e
        g[e:] += 1.0-h[:m]
        g[:m] -= h[e:]
    g *= dx
    return g
```
Check for d=0: first g[:n]+=1-h, second g[:n]-=h ->1-2h.
For d=n-1: m=1: g[:1]+=1-h[n-1], g[n-1:]-=h[:1]. Good.
For d=-(n-1): e=n-1,m=1: g[e:]+=1-h[:1] (only last), g[:1]-=h[e:]. Good.

`soft_lse(g, alpha)`:
```
def soft_lse(g, alpha):
    z=alpha*g
    z-=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    return z.max()/alpha + np.log(s)/alpha, e/s
```
`z.max()` after subtract =0, so F = log(s)/alpha. But if alpha huge and g max large, z max 0; F = log(sum exp)/alpha, which approximates max + log(K)/alpha? Wait since we subtracted max from z, `z.max()=0`, so `LSE(alpha g) = (z.max_original + log(s))/alpha = (max(αg)+log(s))/alpha = max(g)+log(s)/alpha`. We return `z.max()/alpha` after shift =0, missing max(g)! Need fix.
Correct:
```
m = g.max()
z = alpha*(g-m)
e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
F = m + np.log(s)/alpha
w=e/s
return F,w
```
Derivative wrt g: e/s. Good. Use m to avoid large. For α, if g all -? no.

`logsum_obj(x, alpha, n)`:

F,_=soft_lse(corr_values(x,n), alpha); return F
```
`logsum_jac(x, alpha, n)`:

g=corr_values(x,n); _,w=soft_lse(g, alpha)
gr=np.zeros(n,float)
n1=n-1
for idx in range(len(w)):
    wi=w[idx]
    if wi > 1e-12:
       gr += wi * grad_lag(x, idx-n1, n)
return gr
```
This loops all idx. For speed, filter weights above threshold before loop:
```
acts=np.nonzero(w > 1e-10)[0]
for idx in acts: gr += w[idx]*grad_lag(...)
```
For α large, few. For α small up to 30, all; loops 799.
Use `x` in grad_lag as h. Good.

`slsqp_logsum(init,n,alpha,maxiter,ftol)`:
```
def slsqp_logsum(init,n,alpha,maxiter,ftok):
    x=np.asarray(init,float).copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':lo_eq_fun,'jac':lo_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(logsum_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=logsum_jac, args=(alpha,n), constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol,'disp':False})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          return h
    except Exception: pass
    return init
```
No kwargs? okay.

Active functions:

`act_eq_fun(x,n): return x[:n].sum()-0.5*n`
`act_eq_jac(x,n): g=zeros(n+1); g[:n]=1; return g`
`act_ineq_fun(x,n,ds)`:

idx=np.asarray(ds,dtype=int)+n-1

`project_feasible` bisection:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    target=0.5*n
    y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    ymin=y.min(); ymax=y.max()
    lo=ymin-1.0; hi=ymax+1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0)))
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    yp=np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-yp.sum()
    if diff>1e-14:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-yp)); room=1.0-yp[idx]
       yp[idx]=min(1.0, yp[idx]+diff) if room>0 else yp[idx]
    elif diff<-1e-14:
       idx=int(np.argmax(yp)); yp[idx]=max(0.0, yp[idx]+diff)
    return yp
```
If diff cannot be fixed by one index due to many at bounds? Sum target within [0,n]; if target n/2 feasible, bisection should yield sum target to tolerance. Residual maybe very small. One index okay. If room insufficient maybe choose next? Could loop over indices.

```

`to_binary(h,n)`:
```
def to_binary(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if len(h)!=n:
       x0=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       h=np.interp(x1,x0,h)
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hb=np.zeros(n,float)
    hb[idx[:n//2]]=1.0
    return hb
```

def random_binary(n,seed):

    idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False)

```
def swap_delta(i,j,oms,hrev,n):
    N=2*n-1; delta=np.zeros(N,float)
    delta[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] += oms
    delta[i:i+n] -= hrev
    delta[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] -= oms
    delta[j:j+n] += hrev
    delta[n-1] -= 2.0
    if i != j:
       delta[n-1 + j - i] += 1.0
       delta[n-1 + i - j] += 1.0
    return delta
```
But allocation every candidate. For local, we can use preallocated and a function to fill? Function call overhead. Could write inline in loops. To keep code concise, maybe helper `apply_swap_delta(delta,i,j,oms,hrev,n)` fills preallocated. But it does slices. Top-level:

def fill_swap_delta(delta,i,j,oms,hrev,n):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    delta[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] += oms
    delta[i:i+n] -= hrev
    delta[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] -= oms

```
def binary_local(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=1000,seed=1):
    h=to_binary(h,n).copy()
    N=2*n-1; dx=2.0/n
    counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur=counts.max(); best=cur; besth=h.copy()
    oms=1.0-h; hrev=h[::-1].copy()
    zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0].tolist(); ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0].tolist()
    delta=np.empty(N,float); tmp=np.empty(N,float)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       zlist=zeros.copy(); olist=ones.copy(); rng.shuffle(zlist); rng.shuffle(olist)
       improved=False
       for i in zlist:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          for j in olist:
             fill_swap_delta(delta,i,j,oms,hrev,n)
             np.add(counts,delta,out=tmp)
             nc=tmp.max()
             if nc < cur - 1e-12:
                counts += delta; cur=nc
                h[i],h[j]=1,0
                oms[i]=0.0; oms[j]=1.0
                hrev[n-1-i]=1.0; hrev[n-1-j]=0.0
                zeros.remove(i); ones.remove(j); zeros.append(i); ones.append(j)
                improved=True
                if cur < best:
                   best=cur; besth=h.copy()
                break
          if improved: break
       if not improved: break
    # Snapping after many accepted? If drift, recompute counts maybe
    counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cm=counts.max()
    if cm < best:
       besth=h.copy(); best=cm
    return besth, float(best*dx)
```

```
def upsample(h,new_n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if len(h)==1: return np.full(new_n,h[0])
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h))
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,new_n)
    return np.interp(x1,x0,h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
```

Let's write code compactly. We must keep line count. Let's draft final code in analysis, count approximate.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def corr_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1] * (2.0 / n)

def grad_lag(h, d, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, float)
    if d >= 0:
        m = n - d
        g[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
        g[d:] -= h[:m]
    else:
        e = -d
        m = n - e
        g[e:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
        g[:m] -= h[e:]
    g *= dx
    return g

def project_feasible(y, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    y = np.asarray(y, float).copy()
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    yp = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - yp.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        room = 1.0 - yp
        rs = room.sum()
        if rs > 1e-14:
            yp += diff / rs * room
    elif diff < 0:
        hs = yp.sum()
        if hs > 1e-14:
            yp += diff / hs * yp
    return np.clip(yp, 0.0, 1.0)
```
Bisection plus proportional adjustment could violate bounds slightly; final clip. But proportional adjustment may break sum again. Since diff tiny, ok. Could adjust one index to restore; proportional may push beyond bounds if diff not tiny. But after bisection diff < 1e-12 likely. Good. We then clip. Sum may slightly off due clip. Accept.
Maybe use one-index adjust after proportional? Not necessary.

`soft_lse`:
```
def soft_lse(g, alpha):
    m = float(g.max())
    z = alpha * (g - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    return m + np.log(s) / alpha, e / s
```
If alpha huge, z max 0, exp values for g close. If g all? sum≥1.

`logsum_obj`:
```
def logsum_obj(x, alpha, n):
    return float(soft_lse(corr_values(x, n), alpha)[0])
```
`logsum_jac`:
```
def logsum_jac(x, alpha, n):
    g = corr_values(x, n)
    _, w = soft_lse(g, alpha)
    gr = np.zeros(n, float)
    base = n - 1
    for idx in np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]:
        gr += w[idx] * grad_lag(x, int(idx) - base, n)
    return gr
```
`np.nonzero` returns array. Good.

Equality constraints for logsum:
`def lo_eq_fun(x,n): return float(x.sum() - 0.5*n)`
`def lo_eq_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n, float)`

`slsqp_logsum` with bounds; `maxiter` optional; if alpha small and jac slow, okay.
```
def slsqp_logsum(init,n,alpha,maxiter,ftol):
    x = np.asarray(init, float).copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': lo_eq_fun, 'jac': lo_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(logsum_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                       jac=logsum_jac, args=(alpha, n), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init
```

Active funcs:
```
def act_obj(x, n):
    if not np.isfinite(x[n]):
        return 1.0
    return float(x[n])
def act_obj_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[n] = 1.0
    return g
def act_eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 0.5 * n)
def act_eq_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[:n] = 1.0
    return g
def act_ineq_fun(x, n, ds):
    idx = np.asarray(ds, dtype=int) + n - 1
    return x[n] - corr_values(x[:n], n)[idx]
def act_ineq_jac(x, n, ds):
    m = len(ds)
    J = np.zeros((m, n + 1), float)
    h = x[:n]
    for r, d in enumerate(ds):
        J[r, :n] = -grad_lag(h, int(d), n)
        J[r, n] = 1.0
    return J
```
`act_ineq_fun` returns 1D array length m, t - g_l. If m=1, ok.

`active_optimize`:
```
def active_optimize(init, n, ds, maxiter, ftol):
    if len(ds) == 0:
        return init
    g = corr_values(init, n)
    t0 = float(g.max()) + 1e-5
    x = np.empty(n + 1, float)
    x[:n] = init
    x[n] = min(max(t0, 0.0), 1.0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': act_eq_fun, 'jac': act_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)},
            {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': act_ineq_fun, 'jac': act_ineq_jac, 'args': (n, ds)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(act_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                       jac=act_obj_jac, args=(n,), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            h = project_feasible(res.x[:n], n)
            if compute_c5(h, n) < 1.0:
                return h
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init
```
If `compute_c5` >1 return init? C can be >1? C max ≤1? Overlap of measure1 and complement measure ≤1? In continuous integral ≤1. Discrete ≤1 maybe. So if >1 something wrong. But no need.

`to_binary`, `random_binary`, `upsample`, `fill_swap_delta`, `binary_local`, `binary_anneal`, `binary_phase`.
```
def upsample(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    if h.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, h[0])
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
```
`to_binary` use upsample:
```
def to_binary(h, n):
    h2 = upsample(h, n)
    idx = np.argsort(h2)[::-1]
    hb = np.zeros(n, float)
    hb[idx[: n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb
```
For n even. If n odd, target n/2 not integer; but we use even.
`random_binary`:
```
def random_binary(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    idx = rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)
    hb = np.zeros(n, float)
    hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb
```
`fill_swap_delta` as above.
`binary_local`:
Use `max_sweeps` parameter. For candidate list shuffling each sweep, need `rng`. If no time to shuffle? okay.
```
def binary_local(h, n, deadline, max_sweeps=200, seed=1):
    h = to_binary(h, n).copy()
    N = 2 * n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / n
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur = float(counts.max())
    best = cur
    besth = h.copy()
    oms = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].tolist()
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].tolist()
    delta = np.empty(N, float)
    tmp = np.empty(N, float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for _ in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        zlist = zeros.copy()
        olist = ones.copy()
        rng.shuffle(zlist)
        rng.shuffle(olist)
        improved = False
        for i in zlist:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            for j in olist:
                fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n)
                np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)
                nc = float(tmp.max())
                if nc < cur - 1e-12:
                    counts += delta
                    cur = nc
                    h[i], h[j] = 1.0, 0.0
                    oms[i] = 0.0; oms[j] = 1.0
                    hrev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0; hrev[n - 1 - j] = 0.0
                    zeros.remove(int(i)); ones.remove(int(j))
                    zeros.append(int(i)); ones.append(int(j))
                    if cur < best:
                        best = cur; besth = h.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cm = float(counts.max())
    if cm < best:
        best = cm; besth = h.copy()
    return besth, best * dx
```
But note after many swaps, counts may drift; recomputing only at end. If drift causes false improvement before end, besth maybe wrong? To ensure correct, after each accept counts exact? Our delta formula exact for counts if h old. We update h/oms/hrev. Should be exact to floating. No drift if no rounding? It's integer sums but updates using floats; repeated updates may accumulate small errors (1e-12). Could recompute if `if it%1000?` But local accepts likely <1000. Fine.
`zeros.remove` may fail if i not in list? We append accepted zero; lists maintained. Good.

`binary_anneal`:
```
def binary_anneal(h, n, deadline, seed=1):
    h = to_binary(h, n).copy()
    N = 2 * n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / n
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur = counts.max()
    curC = cur * dx
    bestC = curC
    besth = h.copy()
    oms = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    delta = np.empty(N, float)
    tmp = np.empty(N, float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T = 0.02
    cool = 0.99995
    for _ in range(10000000):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        ai = rng.randint(zeros.size)
        bi = rng.randint(ones.size)
        i = int(zeros[ai]); j = int(ones[bi])
        fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n)
        np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)
        nc = float(tmp.max())
        ncC = nc * dx
        if ncC <= curC or (T > 1e-12 and (ncC - curC) < 20.0 * T and rng.random() < np.exp(-(ncC - curC) / T)):
            counts += delta
            cur = nc
            curC = ncC
            h[i], h[j] = 1.0, 0.0
            oms[i] = 0.0; oms[j] = 1.0
            hrev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0; hrev[n - 1 - j] = 0.0
            zeros[ai] = j
            ones[bi] = i
            if curC < bestC:
                bestC = curC
                besth = h.copy()
        T *= cool
    counts = np.correlate(besth, 1.0 - besth, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cm = float(counts.max()) * dx
    if cm < bestC:
        bestC = cm
    return besth, bestC
```
Need if `zeros.size` zero? Binary with n/2 ones and zeros, size>0. Good.
Cooling: if T decays too fast, after 100k T ~0.0015; okay. Maybe better `cool=0.99999` to keep longer. But no matter.

def binary_phase(besth, bestc, deadline, seed):
    besth = np.asarray(besth, float).copy()
    for n in [100, 200, 300, 400]:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        rem = max(2.0, min(45.0, deadline - time.time() + 0.0))
        # allocate slice: use 1/4 of total time left capped
        piece = time.time() + min(35.0, max(5.0, (deadline - time.time()) / 4.0))
        if n == 400:
            # maybe more time if time left large
            piece = time.time() + min(45.0, max(8.0, (deadline - time.time()) / 3.0))
        if piece <= time.time(): continue
        hb = to_binary(besth, n)
        hl, cl = binary_local(hb, n, piece - 1.0, max_sweeps=100 if n <= 200 else 40, seed=seed + n)
        ha, ca = binary_anneal(hl, n, piece, seed=seed + 1000 + n)
        if cl < bestc:
            bestc, besth = cl, hl
        if ca < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ca, ha
        # Also try a random binary if still time and bestc > 0.381?
        if time.time() < deadline - 2 and bestc > 0.381:
            hr = random_binary(n, seed + 7777 + n)
            hr, cr = binary_anneal(hr, n, deadline, seed=seed + 8888 + n) # but this may consume all remaining? Need not.
```

def noise_phase(h,n,deadline,seed):
   best=h.copy(); c=compute_c5(h,n)
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   for sig in [0.05, 0.15, 0.3]:
      if time>deadline: break
      hn=project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0,sig,n), n)
      # quick logsum low
      hh=slsqp_logsum(hn,n,200,120,1e-11)
      hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
      if ch<c: c,best=ch,hh.copy()
   return best,c

Now, continuous_phase code:
```
def continuous_phase(init, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(init, n).copy()
    best = h.copy()
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    # logsum smoothing
    alphas = [40, 120, 400] if n <= 500 else [150, 600]
    lmax = 200 if n <= 500 else 100
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 40:
            break
        hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, lmax, 1e-12)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c, best = ch, hh.copy()
    # active set
    kmax = 20 if n <= 500 else 12
    for k in [6, 10, kmax]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30:
            break
        improved_round = False
        for rep in range(2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 25:
                break
            g = corr_values(best, n)
            C = float(g.max())
            order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
            near = np.where(g >= C - 0.004)[0]
            idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:k], near[:int(2.0*k+4)]))).astype(int)
            # cap
            idxs = idxs[:min(30, int(2.0*k+8))]
            ds = tuple(int(ix) - (n - 1) for ix in idxs)
            hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, 120 if n <= 500 else 70, 1e-12)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c, best = ch, hh.copy()
                improved_round = True
        if not improved_round:
            break
    # high logsum
    for alpha in ([1500, 8000] if n <= 500 else [1500]):
        if time.time() > deadline - 12:
            break
        hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, 150 if n <= 500 else 80, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c, best = ch, hh.copy()
    # final active top 6
    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        g=corr_values(best,n); C=g.max(); order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]; idxs=order[:8].astype(int)
        ds=tuple(int(ix)-(n-1) for ix in idxs)
        hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,80,1e-13); project; if improves update
    return best, c
```

```
def pen_obj(x, alpha, n, pen):
    F,_=soft_lse(corr_values(x,n),alpha)
    s=x.sum()-0.5*n
    return F + 0.5*pen*s*s
def pen_jac(x, alpha, n, pen):
    g=corr_values(x,n); _,w=soft_lse(g,alpha)
    gr=zeros(n); for active w ...
    s=x.sum()-0.5*n
    gr += pen*s
    return gr
def lbfgs_logsum(init,n,alpha,pen,maxiter):
   res=minimize(pen_obj, init, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(0,1)]*n, jac=pen_jac, args=(alpha,n,pen), options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16})
   project_feasible
```

Let's formulate `continuous_phase` precisely:
```
def continuous_phase(init, n, deadline, low=True):
    h = project_feasible(init, n).copy()
    best = h.copy()
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if low:
        if n <= 500:
            alphas = [40, 120, 400]
            lmax = 220
        elif n <= 700:
            alphas = [150, 500]
            lmax = 120
        else:
            alphas = [300]
            lmax = 80
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 45:
                break
            hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, lmax, 1e-12)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch; best = hh.copy()
    elif n > 600:
        # use L-BFGS-B for large polynomial? Maybe call lbfgs
        for alpha in [200, 800, 2500]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 45: break
            hh = lbfgs_logsum(best, n, alpha, 1000.0, 250)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c=ch; best=hh.copy()
```
But if low=True for n=800 we use slsqp logsum [300] 80 iter. Good. If low=False for n>600, use L-BFGS. For 600 upsample, maybe low=True. For 800, low=False. Main initial low=True. Final maybe low=False? We call continuous_phase final? We'll decide.

Binary phase code more precise:
```
def binary_phase(besth,bestc,deadline,seed):
  besth=np.asarray(besth,float).copy()
  for n in [100,200,300,400]:
    if time.time() > deadline: break
    # allocate 20-30 seconds each but not past deadline
    piece = time.time() + min(30.0, max(3.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.25))
    if piece <= time.time()+3: continue
    hb=to_binary(besth,n)
    # local
    hl, cl = binary_local(hb,n, min(piece, deadline) - 2.0, max_sweeps=80 if n>200 else 200, seed=seed+n)
    # anneal remaining piece
    ha, ca = binary_anneal(hl,n, min(piece,deadline), seed=seed+100+n)
    if cl < bestc: bestc,besth=cl,hl.copy()
    if ca < bestc: bestc,besth=ca,ha.copy()
    # if still above 0.381, try random start with short 5s
    if bestc > 0.381 and time.time() + 5 < deadline:
       hr = random_binary(n, seed+777+n)
       hr,cr = binary_anneal(hr,n,deadline,seed=seed+999+n) # This may consume rest? Need slice
```
The random `binary_anneal` call with `deadline` would consume all remaining for that n. To limit, use `piece2 = time.time() + min(8.0, deadline-time.time())` and pass piece2. Add:
```
       rdl = time.time() + min(8.0, max(1.0, deadline - time.time()))
       hr,cr=binary_anneal(hr,n,rdl,seed=...)
```
Good.

def project_feasible(y, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    y = np.asarray(y, float).copy()
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0)))
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    t=0.5*(lo+hi)
    yp=np.clip(y-t,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-yp.sum()
    if diff > 1e-14:
       room=1.0-yp; rs=room.sum()
       if rs > 1e-14: yp += (diff/rs)*room
    elif diff < -1e-14:
       hs=yp.sum()
       if hs > 1e-14: yp += (diff/hs)*yp
    yp=np.clip(yp,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-yp.sum()
    if diff > 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-yp)); yp[idx]=min(1.0, yp[idx]+diff)
    elif diff < 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(yp)); yp[idx]=max(0.0, yp[idx]+diff)
    return yp
```

def fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    s1 = n - 1 - i

Draft:
```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def corr_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1] * (2.0 / n)

def grad_lag(h, d, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, float)
    if d >= 0:
        m = n - d
        g[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
        g[d:] -= h[:m]
    else:
        e = -d
        m = n - e
        g[e:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
        g[:m] -= h[e:]
    g *= dx
    return g

def project_feasible(y, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    y = np.asarray(y, float).copy()
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    yp = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - yp.sum()
    if diff > 1e-14:
        room = 1.0 - yp
        rs = room.sum()
        if rs > 1e-14:
            yp += (diff / rs) * room
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        hs = yp.sum()
        if hs > 1e-14:
            yp += (diff / hs) * yp
    yp = np.clip(yp, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - yp.sum()
    if diff > 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - yp))
        yp[idx] = min(1.0, float(yp[idx] + diff))
    elif diff < 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(yp))
        yp[idx] = max(0.0, float(yp[idx] + diff))
    return yp

def soft_lse(g, alpha):
    m = float(g.max())
    z = alpha * (g - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    return m + np.log(s) / alpha, e / s

def lo_eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)

def lo_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, float)

def logsum_obj(x, alpha, n):
    return float(soft_lse(corr_values(x, n), alpha)[0])

def logsum_jac(x, alpha, n):
    g = corr_values(x, n)
    _, w = soft_lse(g, alpha)
    gr = np.zeros(n, float)
    base = n - 1
    for idx in np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]:
        gr += w[idx] * grad_lag(x, int(idx) - base, n)
    return gr

def slsqp_logsum(init, n, alpha, maxiter, ftol):
    x = np.asarray(init, float).copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': lo_eq_fun, 'jac': lo_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(logsum_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=logsum_jac,
                       args=(alpha, n), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init

def pen_obj(x, alpha, n, pen):
    F = soft_lse(corr_values(x, n), alpha)[0]
    s = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return float(F + 0.5 * pen * s * s)

def pen_jac(x, alpha, n, pen):
    g = corr_values(x, n)
    _, w = soft_lse(g, alpha)
    gr = np.zeros(n, float)
    base = n - 1
    for idx in np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]:
        gr += w[idx] * grad_lag(x, int(idx) - base, n)
    s = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    gr += pen * s
    return gr

def lbfgs_logsum(init, n, alpha, pen, maxiter):
    x = np.asarray(init, float).copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(pen_obj, x, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=pen_jac,
                       args=(alpha, n, pen),
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-15, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init

def act_obj(x, n):
    if not np.isfinite(x[n]):
        return 1.0
    return float(x[n])

def act_obj_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[n] = 1.0
    return g

def act_eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 0.5 * n)

def act_eq_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[:n] = 1.0
    return g

def act_ineq_fun(x, n, ds):
    idx = np.asarray(ds, dtype=int) + n - 1
    return x[n] - corr_values(x[:n], n)[idx]

def act_ineq_jac(x, n, ds):
    m = len(ds)
    J = np.zeros((m, n + 1), float)
    h = x[:n]
    for r in range(m):
        J[r, :n] = -grad_lag(h, int(ds[r]), n)
        J[r, n] = 1.0
    return J

def active_optimize(init, n, ds, maxiter, ftol):
    if len(ds) == 0:
        return init
    g = corr_values(init, n)
    t0 = float(g.max()) + 1e-5
    x = np.empty(n + 1, float)
    x[:n] = init
    x[n] = min(max(t0, 0.0), 1.0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': act_eq_fun, 'jac': act_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)},
            {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': act_ineq_fun, 'jac': act_ineq_jac, 'args': (n, ds)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(act_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=act_obj_jac,
                       args=(n,), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            h = project_feasible(res.x[:n], n)
            return h
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init

def upsample(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, h[0], float)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))

def to_binary(h, n):
    h2 = upsample(h, n)
    idx = np.argsort(h2)[::-1]
    hb = np.zeros(n, float)
    hb[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb

def random_binary(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    idx = rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)
    hb = np.zeros(n, float)
    hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb

def fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    s1 = n - 1 - i
    delta[s1:2 * n - 1 - i] += oms
    delta[i:i + n] -= hrev
    s2 = n - 1 - j
    delta[s2:2 * n - 1 - j] -= oms
    delta[j:j + n] += hrev
    delta[n - 1] -= 2.0
    if i != j:
        delta[n - 1 + j - i] += 1.0
        delta[n - 1 + i - j] += 1.0

def binary_local(h, n, deadline, max_sweeps=200, seed=1):
    h = to_binary(h, n).copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    N = 2 * n - 1
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur = float(counts.max())
    besth = h.copy()
    oms = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].tolist()
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].tolist()
    delta = np.empty(N, float)
    tmp = np.empty(N, float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for _ in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        zlist = zeros.copy()
        olist = ones.copy()
        rng.shuffle(zlist)
        rng.shuffle(olist)
        improved = False
        for i in zlist:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            for j in olist:
                fill_swap_delta(delta, int(i), int(j), oms, hrev, n)
                np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)
                nc = float(tmp.max())
                if nc < cur - 1e-12:
                    counts += delta
                    cur = nc
                    h[int(i)], h[int(j)] = 1.0, 0.0
                    oms[int(i)] = 0.0
                    oms[int(j)] = 1.0
                    hrev[n - 1 - int(i)] = 1.0
                    hrev[n - 1 - int(j)] = 0.0
                    zeros.remove(int(i))
                    ones.remove(int(j))
                    zeros.append(int(i))
                    ones.append(int(j))
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break
    c1 = compute_c5(h, n)
    c2 = compute_c5(besth, n)
    if c2 <= c1:
        return besth, c2
    return h, c1

def binary_anneal(h, n, deadline, seed=1):
    h = to_binary(h, n).copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    N = 2 * n - 1
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur = float(counts.max())
    curC = cur * dx
    besth = h.copy()
    bestC = curC
    oms = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    delta = np.empty(N, float)
    tmp = np.empty(N, float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T = 0.02
    ac = 0
    for _ in range(10000000):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break
        ai = rng.randint(zeros.size)
        bi = rng.randint(ones.size)
        i = int(zeros[ai])
        j = int(ones[bi])
        fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n)
        np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)
        nc = float(tmp.max())
        ncC = nc * dx
        if ncC <= curC or (T > 1e-12 and (ncC - curC) < 20.0 * T and
                           rng.random() < np.exp(-(ncC - curC) / T)):
            counts += delta
            cur = nc
            curC = ncC
            h[i], h[j] = 1.0, 0.0
            oms[i] = 0.0
            oms[j] = 1.0
            hrev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0
            hrev[n - 1 - j] = 0.0
            zeros[ai] = j
            ones[bi] = i
            if curC < bestC:
                bestC = curC
                besth = h.copy()
            ac += 1
            if ac == 10000:
                counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
                cur = float(counts.max())
                curC = cur * dx
                oms = 1.0 - h
                hrev = h[::-1].copy()
                zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].astype(int)
                ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].astype(int)
                ac = 0
                if curC < bestC:
                    bestC = curC
                    besth = h.copy()
        T *= 0.99995
    c1 = compute_c5(h, n)
    c2 = compute_c5(besth, n)
    if c1 < c2:
        return h, c1
    return besth, c2

def binary_phase(besth, bestc, deadline, seed):
    besth = np.asarray(besth, float).copy()
    for n in [100, 200, 300, 400]:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        piece = time.time() + min(30.0, max(4.0, rem * 0.25))
        hb = to_binary(besth, n)
        hl, cl = binary_local(hb, n, min(piece, deadline) - 1.0, max_sweeps=80 if n > 200 else 200, seed=seed + n)
        ha, ca = binary_anneal(hl, n, min(piece, deadline), seed=seed + 100 + n)
        if cl < bestc:
            bestc, besth = cl, hl.copy()
        if ca < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ca, ha.copy()
        if bestc > 0.381 and time.time() + 5.0 < deadline:
            rdl = time.time() + min(8.0, max(1.0, deadline - time.time()))
            hr = random_binary(n, seed + 777 + n)
            hr, cr = binary_anneal(hr, n, rdl, seed=seed + 999 + n)
            if cr < bestc:
                bestc, besth = cr, hr.copy()
    return besth, bestc

def continuous_phase(init, n, deadline, low=True):
    best = project_feasible(init, n).copy()
    c = compute_c5(best, n)
    if low:
        if n <= 450:
            alphas = [40, 120, 400]
            mx = 220
            for alpha in alphas:
                if time.time() > deadline - 45:
                    break
                hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, mx, 1e-12)
                ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
                if ch < c - 1e-12:
                    c = ch
                    best = hh.copy()
        elif n <= 650:
            alphas = [150, 500]
            mx = 120
            for alpha in alphas:
                if time.time() > deadline - 45:
                    break
                hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, mx, 1e-12)
                ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
                if ch < c - 1e-12:
                    c = ch
                    best = hh.copy()
        else:
            alphas = [250, 1000, 3000]
            for alpha in alphas:
                if time.time() > deadline - 45:
                    break
                hh = lbfgs_logsum(best, n, alpha, 1000.0, 250)
                ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
                if ch < c - 1e-12:
                    c = ch
                    best = hh.copy()
    kmax = 22 if n <= 500 else 10
    if n <= 500:
        kseq = [6, 10, kmax]
        mx = 130
    else:
        kseq = [6, 10]
        mx = 70
    for k in kseq:
        if time.time() > deadline - 35:
            break
        imp = False
        for rep in range(2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 30:
                break
            g = corr_values(best, n)
            C = float(g.max())
            order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
            near = np.where(g >= C - 0.004)[0]
            idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:k], near[:int(2 * k + 4)]))).astype(int)
            cap = int(min(30, 2 * k + 8))
            idxs = idxs[:cap]
            ds = []
            for ix in idxs:
                ds.append(int(ix) - (n - 1))
            hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, mx, 1e-12)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
                imp = True
        if not imp:
            continue
    if n <= 450:
        pol = [1500, 8000]
        pmx = 150
        for alpha in pol:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20:
                break
            hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, alpha, pmx, 1e-13)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
    elif n <= 650:
        if time.time() < deadline - 20:
            hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, 1500, 100, 1e-13)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
    else:
        alphas = [1500, 5000]
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20:
                break
            hh = lbfgs_logsum(best, n, alpha, 1000.0, 160)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        g = corr_values(best, n)
        order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        ds = []
        for ix in order[:8]:
            ds.append(int(ix) - (n - 1))
        mx = 80 if n <= 500 else 50
        hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, mx, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c = ch
            best = hh.copy()
    return best, c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    safe_end = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 50.0)
    total = max(60.0, safe_end - t0)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    except Exception:
        n0 = 200
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, float)
    n0 = h0.size
    if n0 > 900:
        h0 = upsample(h0, 800)
        n0 = 800
    h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = compute_c5(h, n0)
    if budget > 400.0:
        db = min(safe_end - 600.0, t0 + 0.10 * total)
        if db > t0 + 10.0:
            besth, bestc = binary_phase(besth, bestc, db, seed)
    dm = min(safe_end - 420.0, t0 + 0.55 * total)
    if time.time() < dm - 25.0:
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(besth, besth.size, dm, True)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    d600 = t0 + 0.75 * total
    if besth.size < 600 and time.time() < d600 - 30.0:
        nh = 600
        hh = project_feasible(upsample(besth, nh), nh)
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(hh, nh, min(d600, safe_end - 260.0), True)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    d800 = t0 + 0.92 * total
    if besth.size < 800 and time.time() < d800 - 30.0:
        nh = 800
        hh = project_feasible(upsample(besth, nh), nh)
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(hh, nh, min(d800, safe_end - 120.0), False)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    if time.time() < safe_end - 20.0:
        ncur = besth.size
        g = corr_values(besth, ncur)
        order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        ds = []
        for ix in order[:10]:
            ds.append(int(ix) - (ncur - 1))
        mx = 80 if ncur <= 500 else 50
        hh = active_optimize(besth, ncur, ds, mx, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, ncur)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, ncur)
        if ch < bestc - 1e-12:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
        if ncur <= 500 and time.time() < safe_end - 10.0:
            hh = slsqp_logsum(besth, ncur, 8000, 80, 1e-13)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, ncur)
            if ch < bestc - 1e-12:
                bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
        else:
            hh = lbfgs_logsum(besth, ncur, 3000, 1000.0, 100)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, ncur)
            if ch < bestc - 1e-12:
                bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    bestn = besth.size
    besth = project_feasible(besth, bestn)
    bestc = compute_c5(besth, bestn)
    return besth, bestc, int(bestn)
```

`project_feasible` can be shorter:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).copy(); t=0.5*n
    lo,hi=y.min()-1,y.max()+1
    for _ in range(60):
      m=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(y-m,0,1).sum()
      if s>t: lo=m
      else: hi=m
    z=np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    d=t-z.sum()
    if d>0:
      r=1-z; z+=d/r.sum()*r if r.sum()>0 else 0
    elif d<0:
      z+=d/z.sum()*z if z.sum()>0 else 0
    z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    d=t-z.sum()
    if d: z[int(np.argmax(1-z if d>0 else z))]=float(np.clip(z[int(np.argmax(1-z if d>0 else z))]+d,0,1))
    return z
```

`project_feasible` more concise but valid:
```
def project_feasible(y,n):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    t=0.5*n
    lo,hi=float(y.min())-1.0,float(y.max())+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        m=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if float(np.sum(np.clip(y-m,0.0,1.0)))>t: lo=m
        else: hi=m
    z=np.clip(y-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    d=t-z.sum()
    if d>0:
        r=1.0-z
        if r.sum()>1e-15: z+=d/r.sum()*r
    elif d<0:
        if z.sum()>1e-15: z+=d/z.sum()*z
    z=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    d=t-z.sum()
    if d>0:
        i=int(np.argmax(1.0-z)); z[i]=min(1.0,float(z[i]+d))
    elif d<0:
        i=int(np.argmax(z)); z[i]=max(0.0,float(z[i]+d))
    return z
```
60 iterations enough.

`grad_lag` as before.

`soft_lse`:
```
def soft_lse(g,a):
    m=float(g.max()); z=a*(g-m); e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum())
    return m+np.log(s)/a, e/s
```

Equality and obj functions compact:
```
def lo_eq(x,n): return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
def lo_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n,float)
def log_obj(x,a,n): return float(soft_lse(corr_values(x,n),a)[0])
def log_jac(x,a,n):
    g=corr_values(x,n); _,w=soft_lse(g,a); gr=np.zeros(n,float); b=n-1
    for i in np.nonzero(w>1e-12)[0]: gr += w[i]*grad_lag(x,int(i)-b,n)
    return gr
```

`binary_local`: Need compact but clear.
```
def binary_local(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps,seed):
    h=to_binary(h,n).copy(); dx=2.0/n; N=2*n-1
    counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur=float(counts.max()); besth=h.copy()
    oms=1.0-h; hrev=h[::-1].copy()
    zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0].tolist(); ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0].tolist()
    delta=np.empty(N,float); tmp=np.empty(N,float); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for _ in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        z=zeros.copy(); o=ones.copy(); rng.shuffle(z); rng.shuffle(o)
        imp=False
        for i in z:
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            for j in o:
                fill_swap_delta(delta,int(i),int(j),oms,hrev,n)
                np.add(counts,delta,out=tmp); nc=float(tmp.max())
                if nc<cur-1e-12:
                    counts+=delta; cur=nc
                    h[int(i)],h[int(j)]=1.0,0.0
                    oms[int(i)]=0.0; oms[int(j)]=1.0
                    hrev[n-1-int(i)]=1.0; hrev[n-1-int(j)]=0.0
                    zeros.remove(int(i)); ones.remove(int(j)); zeros.append(int(i)); ones.append(int(j))
                    imp=True; break
            if imp: break
        if not imp: break
    c1=compute_c5(h,n); c2=compute_c5(besth,n)
    return (besth,c2) if c2<=c1 else (h,c1)
```
Good.

`continuous_phase` compact but lengthy. Write with helper for active selection? To avoid repeated code, can create top-level `select_ds(g,n,k,marg,cap)` returning ds list. This adds function but reduces lines.
```
def select_ds(g,n,k,marg,cap):
    C=float(g.max()); order=np.argsort(g)[::-1]
    near=np.where(g>=C-marg)[0]
    idxs=np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:k],near[:int(2*k+4)]))).astype(int)[:cap]
    ds=[]
    for ix in idxs: ds.append(int(ix)-(n-1))
    return ds
```
This is 7 lines. Use in continuous and run. Good.
Then active selection repeated. Use in continuous.

`continuous_phase`:

def continuous_phase(init,n,deadline,low=True):
    best=project_feasible(init,n).copy(); c=compute_c5(best,n)
    if low:
       if n<=450: alphas=[40,120,400]; mx=220
       elif n<=650: alphas=[150,500]; mx=120
       else: alphas=[250,1000]; mx=80
       for a in alphas:
           if time.time()>deadline-45: break
           hh=slsqp_logsum(best,n,a,mx,1e-12); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
           if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy()
    kseq=[6,10,22] if n<=500 else [6,10]
    mx=130 if n<=500 else 70
    for k in kseq:
       if time.time()>deadline-35: break
       imp=False
       for _ in range(2):
          if time.time()>deadline-30: break
          ds=select_ds(corr_values(best,n),n,k,0.004,min(30,2*k+8))
          if len(ds)==0: break
          hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,mx,1e-12)
          hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
          if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy(); imp=True
    for _ in range(4):
       if time.time()>deadline-25: break
       ds=select_ds(corr_values(best,n),n,10,0.003,20)
       hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,100 if n<=500 else 60,1e-13)
       hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
       if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy()
       else: break
    if n<=450:
       for a in [1500,8000]:
          if time.time()>deadline-20: break
          hh=slsqp_logsum(best,n,a,150,1e-13); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
          if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy()
    elif n<=650 and time.time()<deadline-20:
       hh=slsqp_logsum(best,n,1500,100,1e-13); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
       if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy()
    if time.time()<deadline-15:
       ds=select_ds(corr_values(best,n),n,8,0.0,10)
       hh=active_optimize(best,n,ds,80 if n<=500 else 50,1e-13)
       hh=project_feasible(hh,n); ch=compute_c5(hh,n)
       if ch<c-1e-12: c=ch; best=hh.copy()
    return best,c
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); budget=max(120.0,float(budget_s)); safe=t0+max(60.0,budget-50.0); tot=max(60.0,safe-t0)
    try: h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float).reshape(-1).copy()
    except Exception: h0=np.full(200,0.5,float)
    n0=h0.size
    if n0>900:
       h0=upsample(h0,800); n0=800
       besth=project_feasible(h0,n0).copy(); bestc=compute_c5(besth,n0)
    else:
       hr=np.clip(h0,0.0,1.0); rc=compute_c5(hr,n0)
       if abs(float(hr.sum())-0.5*n0)<1e-8:
          besth=hr.copy(); bestc=rc
       else:
          besth=project_feasible(hr,n0); bestc=compute_c5(besth,n0)
    if budget>400:
       db=min(safe-600.0,t0+0.10*tot)
       if db>t0+10: besth,bestc=binary_phase(besth,bestc,db,seed)
    dm=min(safe-420.0,t0+0.55*tot)
    if time.time()<dm-25:
       hh,ch=continuous_phase(besth,besth.size,dm,True)
       if ch<bestc: bestc,besth=ch,hh.copy()
    d600=t0+0.75*tot
    if besth.size<600 and time.time()<d600-30:
       hh=project_feasible(upsample(besth,600),600)
       hh,ch=continuous_phase(hh,600,min(d600,safe-260.0),True)
       if ch<bestc: bestc,besth=ch,hh.copy()
    d800=t0+0.92*tot
    if besth.size<800 and time.time()<d800-30:
       hh=project_feasible(upsample(besth,800),800)
       hh,ch=continuous_phase(hh,800,min(d800,safe-120.0),False)
       if ch<bestc: bestc,besth=ch,hh.copy()
    if time.time()<safe-20:
       ds=select_ds(corr_values(besth,besth.size),besth.size,10,0.0,12)
       hh=active_optimize(besth,besth.size,ds,80 if besth.size<=500 else 50,1e-13)
       hh=project_feasible(hh,besth.size); ch=compute_c5(hh,besth.size)
       if ch<bestc-1e-12: bestc,besth=ch,hh.copy()
       if besth.size<=500 and time.time()<safe-10:
           hh=slsqp_logsum(besth,besth.size,8000,80,1e-13)
           ch=compute_c5(hh,besth.size)
           if ch<bestc-1e-12: bestc,besth=ch,hh.copy()
    n=besth.size; besth=project_feasible(besth,n); bestc=compute_c5(besth,n)
    return besth,bestc,int(n)
```

def anneal_binary(h,n,deadline,seed):
    h=to_binary(h,n).copy(); dx=2/n; counts=...
    cur=counts.max(); curC=cur*dx; besth=h.copy(); bestC=curC
    oms=1-h; hrev=h[::-1].copy(); zeros=...; ones=...
    delta=np.empty(2*n-1); tmp=...
    rng=...
    T=0.02
    for _ in range(10000000):
       if time>deadline or zeros.size==0: break
       ai=rng.randint(zeros.size); bi=rng.randint(ones.size)
       i=zeros[ai]; j=ones[bi]
       fill_swap_delta(...)
       np.add(counts,delta,out=tmp); nc=tmp.max(); ncC=nc*dx
       if ncC<=curC or (T>1e-12 and (ncC-curC)<20*T and rng.random()<np.exp(-(ncC-curC)/T)):
           counts += delta; cur=nc; curC=ncC; update h/lists
           if curC<bestC: bestC=curC; besth=h.copy()
       T*=0.99995
    c1=compute_c5(h,n); c2=compute_c5(besth,n); return (besth,c2) if c2<=c1 else (h,c1)
```

Time phases: binary 10%, main 55%, 600 25%, 800 17%. If binary doesn't improve, okay.

Let's verify `select_ds` with `marg` negative? no. Use `C-marg` with marg=0.0; near includes all with g>=C (max ties). okay.

Potential issue: In `continuous_phase` active loop `if not imp: continue` means after no imp for k=6, tries k=10, k=22. Good. Then repeated top10 loop 4. Maybe enough.

Potential issue: In run final, if besth.size odd, `active_optimize` works, `to_binary` not used. fine.

Potential issue: If initial n0=400, binary phase `to_binary(besth,n)` upsample if n different. For n=100, linear interpolation then top half. Good.

Let's consider if `np.correlate` on binary arrays creates integer? We cast float. good.

Now, final code with no L-BFGS; check line count maybe under 300. Let's write it carefully.

One possible bug: In `act_ineq_jac`, `ds` can be a list; `for r in range(m): J[r,:n] = -grad_lag(h, int(ds[r]), n)`. Good.

One possible bug: In `slsqp_logsum`, `minimize` with `args=(alpha,n)` will pass to `log_obj` and `log_jac`. It may also pass to constraints? No, constraints have own args. good.

One possible bug: In `act_obj_jac`, returns gradient of objective x[n] wrt x. Good. But objective `act_obj` takes `(x,n)`, returns x[n]. The derivative wrt x[0:n] zeros, x[n]=1. Good.

One possible bug: SLSQP with `constraints` inequality expects `fun` returns array of values where `>=0`. We return `t - g_l`. Good.

One possible bug: `act_eq_jac` returns 1D length n+1. SciPy may expect shape (1,n+1). Should be okay. If not, could fail. To be safe, return `J[0,:]`? 1D accepted. In previous SLSQP equality with vector jac is standard. Fine.

One possible bug: In `project_feasible`, if `d > 0` after final clip, we choose `argmax(1-z)` and add diff. If diff larger than room, clip to 1 and sum still low; but diff small. okay.

Let's now prepare final code.

Double-check no nested functions/lambdas/generators. The code uses:
- no lambda.
- no list comprehension? `bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n` list literal repeated, no comprehension. `kseq = [6,10,22]` literal. `for a in alphas`. `for ix in idxs: ds.append`. No generator. Good.
- `args=(alpha,n)` tuple.
- No closures.

Potential issue: `except Exception` broad okay.

Let's think if initial_h_values global exists in namespace; harness prepends. In run, `np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)` if not defined catches. Good.

Now, final answer only fenced code. Ensure code starts with ```python and ends.

Before final, consider if target 0.3808 but our returned bestc maybe after final project update. If project final increases c, we recompute and return that. Good.

Potential improvement: If final project after all improves h? It could increase c if h not feasible (but active already project). If final project increases, bestc updates to increased. But besth prior feasible, project shouldn't change much. If it changes increasing, we lose previous better feasible c? Actually besth already from project, project idempotent; no change. Good.

Let's final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def corr_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1] * (2.0 / n)

def grad_lag(h, d, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, float)
    if d >= 0:
        m = n - d
        g[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
        g[d:] -= h[:m]
    else:
        e = -d
        m = n - e
        g[e:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
        g[:m] -= h[e:]
    g *= dx
    return g

def project_feasible(y, n):
    y = np.asarray(y, float).copy()
    t = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.sum(np.clip(y - m, 0.0, 1.0))) > t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    z = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - z.sum()
    if d > 0:
        r = 1.0 - z
        if r.sum() > 1e-15:
            z += d / r.sum() * r
    elif d < 0:
        if z.sum() > 1e-15:
            z += d / z.sum() * z
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - z.sum()
    if d > 0:
        i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - z))
        z[i] = min(1.0, float(z[i] + d))
    elif d < 0:
        i = int(np.argmax(z))
        z[i] = max(0.0, float(z[i] + d))
    return z

def soft_lse(g, a):
    m = float(g.max())
    z = a * (g - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    return m + np.log(s) / a, e / s

def lo_eq(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)

def lo_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, float)

def log_obj(x, a, n):
    return float(soft_lse(corr_values(x, n), a)[0])

def log_jac(x, a, n):
    g = corr_values(x, n)
    _, w = soft_lse(g, a)
    gr = np.zeros(n, float)
    b = n - 1
    for i in np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]:
        gr += w[i] * grad_lag(x, int(i) - b, n)
    return gr

def slsqp_logsum(init, n, alpha, maxiter, ftol):
    x = np.asarray(init, float).copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': lo_eq, 'jac': lo_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(log_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=log_jac,
                       args=(alpha, n), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init

def act_obj(x, n):
    return float(x[n]) if np.isfinite(x[n]) else 1.0

def act_jac(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[n] = 1.0
    return g

def act_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 0.5 * n)

def act_eqj(x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n + 1, float)
    g[:n] = 1.0
    return g

def act_inf(x, n, ds):
    idx = np.asarray(ds, dtype=int) + n - 1
    return x[n] - corr_values(x[:n], n)[idx]

def act_inj(x, n, ds):
    m = len(ds)
    J = np.zeros((m, n + 1), float)
    h = x[:n]
    for r in range(m):
        J[r, :n] = -grad_lag(h, int(ds[r]), n)
        J[r, n] = 1.0
    return J

def active_optimize(init, n, ds, maxiter, ftol):
    if len(ds) == 0:
        return init
    g = corr_values(init, n)
    t0 = float(g.max()) + 1e-5
    x = np.empty(n + 1, float)
    x[:n] = init
    x[n] = min(max(t0, 0.0), 1.0)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': act_eq, 'jac': act_eqj, 'args': (n,)},
            {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': act_inf, 'jac': act_inj, 'args': (n, ds)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(act_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=act_jac,
                       args=(n,), constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': ftol, 'disp': False})
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            return project_feasible(res.x[:n], n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return init

def select_ds(g, n, k, marg, cap):
    C = float(g.max())
    order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
    near = np.where(g >= C - marg)[0]
    idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:k], near[:int(2 * k + 4)]))).astype(int)
    idxs = idxs[:int(cap)]
    ds = []
    for ix in idxs:
        ds.append(int(ix) - (n - 1))
    return ds

def upsample(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, h[0], float)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))

def to_binary(h, n):
    h2 = upsample(h, n)
    idx = np.argsort(h2)[::-1]
    hb = np.zeros(n, float)
    hb[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb

def fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    s1 = n - 1 - i
    delta[s1:2 * n - 1 - i] += oms
    delta[i:i + n] -= hrev
    s2 = n - 1 - j
    delta[s2:2 * n - 1 - j] -= oms
    delta[j:j + n] += hrev
    delta[n - 1] -= 2.0
    if i != j:
        delta[n - 1 + j - i] += 1.0
        delta[n - 1 + i - j] += 1.0

def anneal_binary(h, n, deadline, seed=1):
    h = to_binary(h, n).copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    N = 2 * n - 1
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1].astype(float)
    cur = float(counts.max())
    curC = cur * dx
    besth = h.copy()
    bestC = curC
    oms = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].astype(int)
    delta = np.empty(N, float)
    tmp = np.empty(N, float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T = 0.02
    for _ in range(10000000):
        if time.time() > deadline or zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break
        ai = rng.randint(zeros.size)
        bi = rng.randint(ones.size)
        i = int(zeros[ai])
        j = int(ones[bi])
        fill_swap_delta(delta, i, j, oms, hrev, n)
        np.add(counts, delta, out=tmp)
        nc = float(tmp.max())
        ncC = nc * dx
        if ncC <= curC or (T > 1e-12 and (ncC - curC) < 20.0 * T and
                           rng.random() < np.exp(-(ncC - curC) / T)):
            counts += delta
            cur = nc
            curC = ncC
            h[i], h[j] = 1.0, 0.0
            oms[i] = 0.0
            oms[j] = 1.0
            hrev[n - 1 - i] = 1.0
            hrev[n - 1 - j] = 0.0
            zeros[ai] = j
            ones[bi] = i
            if curC < bestC:
                bestC = curC
                besth = h.copy()
        T *= 0.99995
    c1 = compute_c5(h, n)
    c2 = compute_c5(besth, n)
    return (besth, c2) if c2 <= c1 else (h, c1)

def binary_phase(besth, bestc, deadline, seed):
    besth = np.asarray(besth, float).copy()
    for n in [100, 200, 400]:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 10.0:
            break
        piece = time.time() + min(25.0, max(3.0, rem * 0.30))
        hb = to_binary(besth, n)
        ha, ca = anneal_binary(hb, n, min(piece, deadline), seed + 100 + n)
        if ca < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ca, ha.copy()
    return besth, bestc

def continuous_phase(init, n, deadline, low=True):
    best = project_feasible(init, n).copy()
    c = compute_c5(best, n)
    if low:
        if n <= 450:
            alphas = [40, 120, 400]
            mx = 220
        elif n <= 650:
            alphas = [150, 500]
            mx = 120
        else:
            alphas = [250, 1000]
            mx = 80
        for a in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 45:
                break
            hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, a, mx, 1e-12)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
    kseq = [6, 10, 22] if n <= 500 else [6, 10]
    mx = 130 if n <= 500 else 70
    for k in kseq:
        if time.time() > deadline - 35:
            break
        imp = False
        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 30:
                break
            ds = select_ds(corr_values(best, n), n, k, 0.004, min(30, 2 * k + 8))
            if len(ds) == 0:
                break
            hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, mx, 1e-12)
            hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
                imp = True
    for _ in range(4):
        if time.time() > deadline - 25:
            break
        ds = select_ds(corr_values(best, n), n, 10, 0.003, 20)
        hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, 100 if n <= 500 else 60, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c = ch
            best = hh.copy()
        else:
            break
    if n <= 450:
        for a in [1500, 8000]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20:
                break
            hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, a, 150, 1e-13)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
            if ch < c - 1e-12:
                c = ch
                best = hh.copy()
    elif n <= 650 and time.time() < deadline - 20:
        hh = slsqp_logsum(best, n, 1500, 100, 1e-13)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c = ch
            best = hh.copy()
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        ds = select_ds(corr_values(best, n), n, 8, 0.0, 10)
        hh = active_optimize(best, n, ds, 80 if n <= 500 else 50, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, n)
        if ch < c - 1e-12:
            c = ch
            best = hh.copy()
    return best, c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    safe_end = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 50.0)
    total = max(60.0, safe_end - t0)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(200, 0.5, float)
    n0 = h0.size
    if n0 > 900:
        h0 = upsample(h0, 800)
        n0 = 800
        besth = project_feasible(h0, n0).copy()
        bestc = compute_c5(besth, n0)
    else:
        hr = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
        rc = compute_c5(hr, n0)
        if abs(float(hr.sum()) - 0.5 * n0) < 1e-8:
            besth = hr.copy()
            bestc = rc
        else:
            besth = project_feasible(hr, n0)
            bestc = compute_c5(besth, n0)
    if budget > 400.0:
        db = min(safe_end - 600.0, t0 + 0.10 * total)
        if db > t0 + 10.0:
            besth, bestc = binary_phase(besth, bestc, db, seed)
    dm = min(safe_end - 420.0, t0 + 0.55 * total)
    if time.time() < dm - 25.0:
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(besth, besth.size, dm, True)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    d600 = t0 + 0.75 * total
    if besth.size < 600 and time.time() < d600 - 30.0:
        hh = project_feasible(upsample(besth, 600), 600)
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(hh, 600, min(d600, safe_end - 260.0), True)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    d800 = t0 + 0.92 * total
    if besth.size < 800 and time.time() < d800 - 30.0:
        hh = project_feasible(upsample(besth, 800), 800)
        hh, ch = continuous_phase(hh, 800, min(d800, safe_end - 120.0), False)
        if ch < bestc:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    if time.time() < safe_end - 20.0:
        nb = besth.size
        ds = select_ds(corr_values(besth, nb), nb, 10, 0.0, 12)
        hh = active_optimize(besth, nb, ds, 80 if nb <= 500 else 50, 1e-13)
        hh = project_feasible(hh, nb)
        ch = compute_c5(hh, nb)
        if ch < bestc - 1e-12:
            bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
        if nb <= 500 and time.time() < safe_end - 10.0:
            hh = slsqp_logsum(besth, nb, 8000, 80, 1e-13)
            ch = compute_c5(hh, nb)
            if ch < bestc - 1e-12:
                bestc, besth = ch, hh.copy()
    n = besth.size
    besth = project_feasible(besth, n)
    bestc = compute_c5(besth, n)
    return besth, bestc, int(n)
```
